        All the facts reported, the evidence collected, and the testimonies
support the existence of a phenomenon that cannot be explained by known
natural causes.
        Suddenly, what was observed as a "Target Object" during the day

emitting a kind of pulsating light.

flying object" or UFO) became frequent, day after day, increasing in number
and intensity, causing great concern among the population.
        This phenomenon, which we will call "Xaarar" (a local term), is a
daily occurrence, the result of the work of the *Faroe* (main) Observatory
in the field of astronomy (and possibly other sciences). It is believed that
the population's growing interest in this phenomenon is due to the fact that
it is a local event, visible to everyone.

**Subject:** Report of a strange phenomenon

**Location:** [Lacking specific location]

**Date:** [Not specified]

I witnessed an object flying at an impossible angle, seemingly defying the laws of physics. It moved erratically, almost like a toy out of control. The object was not a known aircraft, and it didn't resemble any known natural phenomenon.

It flew at a high speed, and I couldn't make out any distinct features due to its extreme speed. It was a silver, disk-shaped object, and it seemed to be emitting a soft, blue light.

I observed it for approximately 30 seconds before it suddenly disappeared.

This phenomenon is similar to other reports of unidentified flying objects (UFOs) that I have read about.

II - UFOs in the 1990s
1 9 9 7 UFOs are officially recognized worldwide
--- seg-61f6d47b10199a9ddaa62da1 [needs-review] ---
What exactly is a Crystal Skull?
As the name of these objects implies, they are skulls, approximately modeled on the human skull, made from various types of quartz crystal.
There are three types of Crystal Skulls:
- Contemporary ones, which are being made predominantly in Mexico, Brazil, the USA, Germany, etc...
- Old ones, defined as Crystal Skulls made approximately 500 years ago or more.
- Ancient ones, considered more interesting. These ancient artifacts seem to possess a powerful energy that strongly affects people in their presence. In the case of some of these skulls, scientists, archaeologists and researchers are not certain they can explain exactly how they were made.
Some Existing Ancient Crystal Skulls
The famous "Mitchell-Hedges" Crystal Skull, human-sized and shaped, clear quartz, 11.7 pounds, discovered in Belize in 1924 in the ruins of a Maya city.
"Max," the Texas Crystal Skull, simple clear quartz, 18 pounds, kept by the Parks family of Houston, who received the skull from a Tibetan healer.
"ET," a skull carved from a single piece of smoky quartz, discovered on the property of a Maya family in Guatemala in 1906. This is the only human-sized skull made of smoky quartz.
"Paris," a single piece of clear quartz, kept in the Trocadero Museum in Paris, probably obtained by a mercenary in Mexico.
To illustrate this invitation to the lecture on UFOs, we publish texts by one of Brazil's leading researchers since 1969: Pedro Cunha, a friend directly responsible for Shapiro's visit to Brazil.
"In 1979, during the 1st International Ufology Congress held in Brasília, Dr. James Hurtak, Ph.D., a NASA consultant on matters involving dead languages and astroarchaeology, said that 200 thousand objects approached Saturn and disappeared near its rings. Around the world, a veritable flock of UFOs in the most varied shapes astonishes even unwary observers who dare to look at the skies, not to mention Hollywood films: ET, ID4, Alien, etc...
In 1989/90, there was a wave of UFOs over Europe. What happened there? The Belgian armed forces even issued an official statement that the UFOs were of completely unknown origin."
In this lecture, Shapiro will present facts such as these, helping anyone interested in a greater understanding of New Ages; for many years, events involving flying saucers were manipulated by government bodies in various countries and treated superficially and dismissively by the mainstream press.
But we have reached the threshold of humanity's transition to other sources of higher knowledge, and thus we cannot remain indifferent to the accounts of thousands of human beings affected by the problem, left entirely unprotected, disoriented and at the mercy of ridicule and obscure comments from ill-informed people.
The time has come to lift the veil of Isis, based on facts and in the light of science as far as it can take us; to carry wherever possible this reality that has always been present in humanity's history, and for which ufologists, through their pioneering and futuristic work, struggle to secure a space such as this.

"The X-Files" was a phenomenon
on American television in the
1990s. It gained prominence in
prime time, won several Emmy
awards, and garnered a legion of
fans worldwide.
Created by Chris Carter, the
series features two FBI agents:
Fox Mulder, whose sister was
mysteriously abducted and be-
lieves in the existence of extra-
terrestrials, and Dana Scully,
assigned to be Mulder's partner.
Together, they investigate un-
common cases.
They are accompanied by im-
portant supporting characters,
such as the "informant" Deep
Throat.
The main theme is always the
mystery behind the crimes.
Conspiracy is also a frequent
element, as all evidence found
by the agents is mysteriously
diverted to massive files with
no names in a secret FBI warehouse.
Today, after four seasons on
air, "The X-Files" has spawned
novels, trading cards, and made
actors Gillian Anderson (Scully)
and David Duchovny (Mulder)
sexual icons.

Ufologist Cláudio Suenaga, au-

--- seg-2065c2275d629597e66d720f [needs-review] ---
Within the government, the main
reference center for unidentified
flying objects is located in the Ministry of Aeronautics, in the Core of the Brazilian Aerospace Defense Command, known by the acronym Nucomdabra.
The way this core operates is the subject of much debate. UFOlogists swear that Nucomdabra investigates UFO appearances, dispatches agents to locations where they may have appeared, and monitors Brazilian airspace for suspicious movements.
The Aeronautics denies it. "What we do is receive information and archive, archive, archive. Why don't we investigate? Because there is no specific directive in that sense," says Brigadier José Montgomeri Rebouças, head of the Social Communication Center of the Ministry of Aeronautics.
"It must be a fascinating topic because we receive a lot of information, but we do not give scientific treatment to these reports, nor do we consider flying saucers as an aerial threat," he says.
--- seg-981fd59e9d04d1916f4872e0 [needs-review] ---
Preliminary opinion
In response, then-Major Aviator Mardem José de Andrade, of Nucomdabra, sent Suenaga a "preliminary opinion" on the photos, in which he says the light seen in the sky "appears to be a condensation trail (jet stream) associated with an aircraft at high altitude."
Andrade also sent a standard questionnaire requesting information about the UFO, such as the "object's position," its shape, size, color, speed and trail, its trajectory and the duration of the observation.
The questionnaire, with 14 items, is printed on paper without letterhead, as is the signed letter from Major Aviator Mardem de Andrade. (MSy)

MAURICIO STYCER
Local Reporting
The Brazilian military regime (1964-1985) investigated alleged flying-saucer sightings in the 70s and spied on the activities of Brazilian specialists in ETs (extraterrestrials).
Documents from the former Dops (Department of Political and Social Order), now held in the São Paulo State Archive, show that the so-called intelligence services wasted time and money investigating the "abduction" of a São Paulo merchant by the occupants of an unidentified flying object (UFO).
The documents also show that Dops summoned two ufologists, people who study UFOs, to testify and infiltrated an agent into the regular meetings of a group of flying-saucer enthusiasts.
Located by historian Cláudio Tsuyoshi Suenaga, the documents confirm something Brazilian ufologists had always suspected, but which the scientific community regarded as another symptom of the persecution complex afflicting many of these researchers: "The military has always been concerned with the UFO phenomenon," says Suenaga.
"The X-Files"
The historian, who is preparing a master's thesis on the subject at Unesp (São Paulo State University), goes further: "Clearly there is a Brazilian 'X-File.' Even today, the Brazilian government remains concerned with this subject."
Suenaga is referring to the American science-fiction series "The X-Files," broadcast in Brazil by Record and Fox.
The program tells the story of two FBI agents investigating supposedly inexplicable cases (UFOs, ETs, paranormal phenomena, etc.), filed under the heading "X-File."
The FBI (the American secret service) has never confirmed the existence of such a file — a fact that helps fuel the
Physician Max Berezovsky in 74, when he belonged to the Brazilian Association for the Study of Extraterrestrial Civilizations and testified to police about the Patero case in Guarantã
Local Reporting
Dops's "extraterrestrial" investigation originated in an incident on April 28, 1974, near Guarantã (423 km northwest of São Paulo).
That day, according to a report sent to the director of Dops by police chief Hermínio José Theodoro, "Guarantã was shaken by news that the individual Onilson Patero
[...]
ney in a flying saucer, Patero was in the company of four "researchers from the Association for the Study of UFOs."
The police chief hastens to identify the researchers and asks Dops to investigate them, seeking to help establish whether Onilson Patero really traveled in a flying saucer from Guarantã to Colatina.
Romeu Tuma
[...]
almost six months after the Patero case appeared in the newspapers.
"The Invaders"
Physician Berezovsky, still an active ufologist in São Paulo, stated in his testimony that he considered Onilson Patero's account of his first encounter with a flying saucer to be true, but saw signs that the second encounter had been invented.
[...]
that Onilson Patero is a "mythomaniac" who "exhibits a certain neurological alteration."
Reversal
His report is sent to police chief Romeu Tuma, who forwards it to the then director-general of Dops, Lúcio Vieira. The case appears closed — but will be reopened.

The Patero case, as it became known, had great media impact. A merchant, established in Catanduva (385 km northwest of São Paulo), he claimed to have had two contacts with UFOs. The first allegedly occurred in May 73, on a highway near Catanduva. In the second "encounter," which caused great commotion, Patero disappeared for six days. The merchant's car was found abandoned on a highway in the interior of São Paulo in the morning of April 29th, and he reappeared after six days on a farm in Colatina, Espírito Santo. In the report he sent to the Dops, the police commissioner Theodoro observes that, when narrating to journalists his story,

commanded by Roberto Quass, at
the time deputy director of the

The SI was then commanded by

(PFL-SP), who, according to a document,
knew about the main investigation
on the UFOs "seen" by merchant
Onilson Patero.
Among the ufologists who were

of the pioneers of ufology in the

It is the delegate Quass who takes

depositions to the police were given
on October 11, 1974, João Quarasma/Folha Imagem
--- seg-650401d0d96195e438ed0c3c [needs-review] ---
According to the meeting, Professor Wirz, already dead, told the police that he considered both "encounters" of Patero with flying saucers as false. According to Wirz, the story told by the merchant from Catanduva was "completely inconsistent, with many details that remind television movies, particularly the series 'The Invaders'". At the police's request, the two ufologists provide information about other ufologists and ask that their statements be classified as "confidential" to "avoid that Mr. Onilson Patero, upon learning of these, exploits the theme once again, drawing attention to himself". By the end of October, Delegate Quass seemed satisfied with the testimonies of Berezovsky Wirz and accepted the conclusion of the investigation.
--- seg-49606ba4efee5241d7c0415b [needs-review] ---
it undergoes a turn — and the São Paulo UFOlogists are now under investigation.

A document with a 2nd Army stamp, sent to the Information Service of the Dops, reports that "there have been suspicious meetings" at Max Berezovsky's home and at an Israeli club in Higienópolis (downtown São Paulo).

At these meetings, "with the idea of holding debates on Extraterrestrial Civilizations (flying saucers), they seek to account for students and other elements, possibly linked to subversion, for discussion and debate against the constituted government."

It is this anonymous report that leads the Dops to infiltrate agents into the meetings of the São Paulo UFOlogists.

Berezovsky is certain that, during that period, all his telephones were tapped and he was under surveillance.
--- seg-84beb869af708d935597be93 [machine-unreviewed] ---
(Reira) discussed the problem of flying saucers, transmitting numerous theories and information on the subject." Later on, the agent informs that "the speaker's position on the existence of such objects, as civilizations from other planets and galaxies, seemed evident, and it was also apparent that the majority of those present were aficionados and believers in the subject."

Finally, the Dops agent informs that ufologists are in campaign for new members, and most importantly, that he did not observe "any political comment, attitude or allusion" at the meeting.

Thus, with the favorable conclusion of the agent, historian Cláudio Suenaga observes, "one of the most bizarre processes driven by the Brazilian state during the period came to an end." (Fabiano Accoril/Folha Image)

Historian Cláudio Suenaga, 26, is also a passionate about extraterrestrial phenomena. Upon finding documents that prove the investigations by the political police, he felt relieved.
"I had heard, in ufology meetings, that many had been investigated by the dictatorship. But it could have been just another ufologist's paranoia," he says.
With a CNPq (National Council for Scientific and Technological Development) scholarship, Suenaga hopes to defend his thesis, provisionally titled "From Myth to Historical Reality — A study on UFO phenomena" by the end of the year.
Suenaga claims to believe in the existence of UFOs. "But not as a belief. I am a researcher, a scholar."

IMMEDIATE AND GENERAL CHARACTERISTICS OF PAPUDA PRISON
--- seg-a420b51d2ba5ab47f4fc155f [machine-unreviewed] ---
0
--- seg-6c9dc4a0c93a15b59ff6c715 [needs-review] ---
Off highway DF 001, at Km 4, lies DF 465, the access road to the Papuda Prison security area. It is 15 Km southeast of Brasília's Plano Piloto, in a valley with an average elevation of 959m. The higher surrounding areas reach an altitude of 1,150 m.
Within the security area are the Internment and Reintegration Center (CIR), which in April 1991 held about 700 prisoners serving terms between 10 and 15 years; the Brasília Custody Unit (NCB), also under the Federal District Civil Police, which held about 400 prisoners awaiting trial; and the 3rd Independent Military Police Company (3ª CPMInd), whose guard force comprised more than 60 men.
It is isolated from the urban area. Most of the area does not have any lighting. Inside the maximum-security prison, CIR, lighting is provided by 10 high-powered floodlights. There are 10 elevated guard towers and ground guard posts.
In this setting, on 11/04/91 from approximately 19 h 10 min to 22 h 40 min, a flying object that was not identified — UFO — was observed. Lieutenant Damasceno and the soldiers on guard that night made the observation. Approximately 25 police officers were at the 3ª CPMInd. The remaining members of the guard force that night were dispersed on other assignments and at guard posts.
What was observed
Lieutenant Damasceno left the 3ª CPMInd and ordered the driver, Private Reinaldo, to prepare the vehicle for a routine patrol of the prison posts. Upon returning to the Company, he noticed a strange object standing out in the sky at an altitude of about 300 or 400 meters ³. He immediately called the guards to look, about 20 police officers at that moment.
According to the testimonies, the object constantly changed colors: blue, red, yellow and a little green. Occasionally it flashed very strongly, turning the entire object red. Observations were made from three different positions, all within the security perimeter. Reports of other witnesses were also received. We determined that they were workers at nearby charcoal kilns.
The week after collecting the testimonies, we traveled to the charcoal kiln area. The high turnover of workers did not allow

===== PAGE 51 [page-69e3af171a3ee9e20dcc64a5] =====
--- seg-94a01ef37f31418047ddc6e0 [needs-review] ---
Find the WFLI. These had already returned to Minas Gerais, their state of origin, and
another was directed towards a coal mine in the state of Mato Grosso.
Observations from the coal mines would provide significant data as they represented
an unusual observation compared to the others.
When drawing the sketches, Sgt Reinaldo requested that the "center of the object be well
defined" because it would flash different colors in various parts of its body at certain
moments, and at that moment, it would be entirely taken over by a red hue from the center.
Lt Damasceno corrected the drawing and confirmed its oval shape, dividing it into
colors. He observed that the apparent position of the object was vertical and the rate
of color change was very high, making it difficult to establish a sequence. "Red was the
only color that didn't appear; suddenly, it took over the entire object. The color change
was very rapid." He added: "This isn't the first time, nor the third time this has
happened here. Talk to the prison staff and you'll see how everyone is afraid of it."
The absence of a larger number of observers at different points and/or angular
positions reduces the accuracy of distance calculations, location, and the actual diameter
of the object. However, in addition to this, the lack of documentary evidence of the
phenomenon (photos, video footage, radar recordings) obviously makes it difficult to
verify and identify certain propositions, as it compromises an in-depth study of the
nature of the phenomenon. Nevertheless, the absence of these data does not diminish the
credibility of the statements that simultaneously attest to the reality of the event.
Despite the insufficiency of data for more precise calculations, we made an estimate based
on what we had, and as a tool for calculation verification, suggesting a real diameter of
20.50m for the observed shape.

--- seg-946f7f23b9fb6aa879f840d6 [machine-unreviewed] ---
As for the object's movements, Damasceno states: "There was no movement; it was stationary, and we moved to a darker area to observe better. Then we returned to the courtyard, looked, and it was no longer there; suddenly, when we looked again, it was already gone. We witnessed this event. The object moved from the first position to the northeast, returned to its initial position, and then moved to another position further northeast and stayed there until around 22:40. The object disappeared from the first position, appeared in a second, returned to the first, and then reappeared in a third position, where it finally stopped. There was no distraction, and I saw everything clearly." (DTI 0,1 and 2)

Initially, the witness described the object as stationary, then it reappeared and moved.

The use of the terms "desaparecer" (disappear) and "retornar" (return) conveys the idea of a continuous process that could have been observed during its movement. The terms "aparecer" (appear), "desaparecer", and "retornar" are consistent with his statement: "There was no distraction, and I didn't see the movements" in such a way that the same form was observed in different positions.

According to the testimony of Lieutenant Damasceno, during the observation period of the object, which took place from approximately 7:10 PM to 10:40 PM, there were four telephone contacts with CINDACTA I. On the first contact, Lieutenant Damasceno was tasked with identifying the object.

The first contact was made by Lieutenant Damasceno shortly before 8:00 PM. The subsequent contacts were made by Sergeant Petrônio of CINDACTA I. In this regard, it can be said that CINDACTA I demonstrated interest in the incident, predisposing itself to monitor the event upon the request of the officer in charge of the prison guard. However, after several attempts, it decided on the easiest route: "Lieutenant, that was a balloon." To the indignation of the witnesses, this was the necessary, definitive, and objective response that the prison security should have given. A response intended to calm the situation and restore the usual state of tranquility required by the system.

The contradictions in the received documentation justify the inconsistency of the response presented by Sergeant Petrônio (CINDACTA I) to Lieutenant Damasceno (3* CPMInd). It also shows that within the MAER, there is no consensus on how to handle this topic.

The witnesses' indignation can thus be expressed: CINDACTA represents the eyes of the air defense system and air traffic control, and at that moment, despite the difficulties in clarifying the situation, it pretended to be blind, rendering the efficiency of its radars useless. Despite having confirmed by telephone that the radar had recorded something, no fighter jet was deployed to investigate what it was.

--- seg-7893e595f9b0f47adac454f4 [needs-review] ---
The clarification note from the Social Command Center of the Ministry of Aeronautics - CECOMSAER, confirms in item I, the "observation of a signal processed by the computers of that center (CINDACTA I), which was not characterized as any aircraft operating in the area."

The time of registration (19:45) by CINDACTA I, falls within the observation period according to witness statements. It does not coincide with the launch time of the meteorological balloon that day, C.MA-BR, formerly CMI, as per OF No. 020/CMDO/020 of July 10, 1991. This document states the launch time for that day at 21:00. The maximum altitude reached was 24,442 meters, under clear skies, with a maximum wind speed of 230750 knots, equivalent to 92.6 km/h. This indicates that a record of an object was made, and this object was neither a meteorological balloon nor a conventional aircraft.

It can be observed that the object was already being observed before the launch of the balloon, with CINDACTA I recording it at 19:45.

The same clarification note, item 2, states that there was a coincidence in the record of the launch of a meteorological balloon by (the former) CM-1, suggesting the possibility that the meteorological balloon was being recorded. This suggestion, as we have seen, is unfounded, as there was no total coincidence of times, the launch of that day occurred at 21:00, and the observation of the phenomenon ranged from 19:10 to 22:40.

Item 3 presents no inconsistencies.

However, item 4 of the same note presents a partial inconsistency regarding the association of the characteristics of meteorological balloons with the characteristics of UFOs*. Such a generalization cannot be made, as meteorological balloons are easily distinguishable from UFOs in most cases. According to many reports and documents (videos), UFOs exhibit sudden maneuvers, extreme speeds, disappear and reappear, and color variations, in this case depending on the observation time, which have little to do with solar reflections on the surfaces of balloons, as stated in OF No. 020/CMDO/020 and witness testimonies.

CINDACTA I equipment, NUCOMDABRA, through OF. 017/CMDO/017, denies such information.

In item II of Of nº 017/CMDO/017 and its attached questionnaire, the Ministry of Aeronautics once again demonstrates or confirms its interest in the subject.
The questionnaire used to collect UFO information contains precise, objective questions aimed at obtaining solid evidence about the phenomenon.
However, as a comprehensive assessment of the phenomenon, the questionnaire falls short. The characteristics observed and reported generally indicate that the UFO phenomenon is elusive and generates complex social interaction. This calls for a more detailed questionnaire addressing the phenomenon's peculiarities and complexities.
An assessment of its objective and subjective characteristics is therefore necessary. In this regard, the Ministry of Aeronautics' interest is not apparent. The report serves only to assess more commonplace aerial sightings.
Thus, although it appears to recognize the phenomenon's existence by creating a data-gathering instrument, its purpose is not to understand the nature of the phenomenon in its entirety. It aims only to obtain technical data that might affect the performance of its functions in real time.
A large amount of data about the phenomenon itself and the resulting social issues is therefore left aside.
Item III again raises the weather-balloon hypothesis, already analyzed through items 2, 4, 5 of the CECOMSAER Clarification Statement, which can be supplemented further.
Characteristics of the Weather Balloon Used on 11/04/91
According to ofício 020/CMDO/020, the weather balloon used on the night of 11/04/91 was manufactured in Japan by KKS, measures 1.20m in diameter and is made of cosmoprene plastic. It weighs 350g and carries 200g of equipment intended to collect temperature, air direction and air speed data in the upper atmosphere. It can reach an altitude of 25,000m and can be driven by winds reaching speeds "equal to or greater than 130kt" (130 knots = 210.76Km/h).
According to the same source, this balloon is inflated with hydrogen and does not carry lamps.

--- seg-a067edeea7a55882930f2edc [needs-review] ---
It is impossible to assert ignorance of the nature of UFO phenomena without undermining any public or private institution. However, we believe it is necessary to honestly explore our limitations, engage in discussion, and adjust our positions regarding this topic.

In the case of MAER, its positions are so intertwined that it becomes difficult to discern if it possesses investigative functions for such events, or what its role is regarding this subject matter.

Are investigative functions related to the UFO topic defined as a regulatory responsibility for a MAER body?

Social expectations regarding MAER are such that, given its interest in airspace (the predominant arena for UFO activity) and its resources in terms of equipment and personnel, the possibility that its regulations do not provide for investigation procedures for such occurrences is not even considered. In light of the confusion mentioned, it is challenging, even for us, to understand the reasons behind statements that appear so well-intentioned:

"Regarding Air Defense, it is fundamentally distinct from the exercise of controlling airspace safely in real time. Occurrences of phenomena, now under discussion, require clear clarification, as they are generally reported a posteriori of the observed event and with very few elements that can provide a more accurate evaluation." (Maj. Brig. do Ar Ronald Eduardo Jaeckel - Ch do EM do COMGAR: 1993)

The fact is, in this case, the communication was made in real time, allowing for safe monitoring. So, why the denial of registration or the association with meteorological balloons, among so many other contradictions, as we have seen previously?

The sincerity and support of authorities leading the mentioned institutions can enable society, through the collective effort of all involved segments with this topic, to assess the actual needs and treatment methods for this urological problem, including legislative or regulatory aspects. This involves pointing out directions and responsibilities for solving and clarifying a situation that has become a progressively growing social issue.

Numerous researchers, in their statements, have consistently demanded a position from the Ministry of Aeronautics regarding the UFO question. These demands seem to indicate disagreement with existing positions.

Liquid water in Europa
--- seg-7c1edf018a6dcde828dee721 [machine-unreviewed] ---
• • Free catalog • •
--- seg-538af3a01fb5d9a4825ac4b4 [machine-unreviewed] ---
(Liquid water on Europa)
--- seg-5542f43213e865f4c763165a [machine-unreviewed] ---
Laboratory authenticated meteorites
--- seg-bfd22c8c19f0515c4737f4c7 [needs-review] ---
Images obtained by the Galileo probe on February 20, 20th, suggest that Europa, one of Jupiter's moons, has a significant amount of liquid water, an ice crust 1 or 2 km deep, and internal volcanic sources. Such evidence greatly increases the possibilities of life, as science presumes the presence of organic compounds there.

Astronomers believe that if there is any form of life on Europa, it must be much older than the earliest terrestrial bioforms.

High-resolution (54 m/pixel) photographs were taken during a flyby that reached 586 km above the surface. They show (side) what appear to be icebergs floating in an ocean covered with ice, according to analysis by Paul Geissler, a researcher at the University of Arizona in the U.S.

Only two craters were located in the frozen sea, indicating it must be less than one million years old, says Clark Chapman of the Southwest Research Institute. The large Pwyll crater (side) provides elements to support the hypothesis that the crust is indeed thin.

Galileo is scheduled to meet Europa again on November 6 during its 11th orbit around Jupiter. The probe's primary mission will end on December 7, but it is expected to be utilized through the end of 1999, when it will have completed eight more passages over Europa.

On April 5, Galileo obtained images of Ganymede and the data is already being evaluated.

Scientists are already gathering ideas for future exploration of Europa. They plan to send new equipment with the primary purpose of measuring the crust and detecting any possible forms of life there. There is talk of sending a probe to the Pwyll crater, where it would perform chemical analyses of the surface and the likely ocean beneath the frozen crust.

Regardless of the results of ongoing investigations, the prospects for extraterrestrial life are already sufficiently encouraging.

(Daily comets)
Ten years ago, Dr. Louis Frank, from
the University of Iowa, USA, caused
a stir in scientific circles by putting
forward a bold hypothesis in his book
Big Splash. He claimed that our planet
is daily invaded by numerous small comets
that release moderate amounts of water
into our atmosphere. The tiny ice comets,
containing oxygen, nitrogen, and carbon,
can produce brief meteorological effects
during their atmospheric entry process.
After many years of criticism, Dr. Frank
returns to scientific commentary with
new evidence, including photographic
data, in support of his thesis. Now,
with the sympathy of several colleagues,
even NASA dares not deny the scien-
tist's claims.

In the past, "powers" interested in absolute secrecy regarding facts concerning the UFO/OVNI phenomenon threatened death to strangers who seemed to know too much about the subject. It is said, for example, that direct and indirect witnesses to the circumstances that led to the so-called Roswell Incident (04.7.1947) in New Mexico, USA, were intimidated never to discuss what they knew with anyone. In this particular case, the threat came from agents of the former Army Air Forces, who seemed to want all details about the alleged crash of an alien spacecraft in the region buried in oblivion. Over time, intelligence strategy and operations experts realized that the threats would no longer have any effect, mainly due to the unstoppable expansion of civilian UFOlogy worldwide. It would simply be impossible to "accidentally" silence the growing contingent of UFOlogists, because some of them had proven to be very well connected with political and military leaders, as well as with the media. Military UFOlogy has existed since the beginning of World War II. Many things
have been discovered since then, and the procedures of intelligence agencies have
undergone a gradual and necessary improvement. The emergence of organized civil UFOlogy at the beginning of the 1950s and its
rapid spread was seen as a great danger by military experts. Some civilians seemed determined to discover everything the military knew, and
of course, they became a nuisance. At that time, it would have been feasible to kill the few
more audacious "talkers" but today, extermination would be expensive, difficult, and inefficient. At some point in the last two decades, the shadowy strategists of secrecy
must have thought: "If it's not productive to annihilate all undesirable UFOlogists,
what should we do to neutralize them?" The answer to the problem must have flowed
with some ease: Discredit them. (continues)

Located over the Santista Lowlands
(Local wave over the Santist Lowlands)
by/by Edison Boaventura Júnior (GUG)
In June, we had a localized wave of unidentified flying object (UFO) sightings in the Santista Lowlands. As early as the first week of the month, near Guarau, in Peruíbe, SP, several fishermen were frightened by mysterious "fireballs" that, on certain occasions, flew close to their boats.
On June 13, around 9:30 PM, Mr. Walter Cunha, a resident of São Vicente, reported seeing an UFO with his daughter as it crossed the sky from east to west. The aeriform had white, intermittent lights arranged in the form of an "M." The phenomenon was observed again on June 16.
In the evening of the same June 13, a square UFO with intense blinking lights was seen by a family leaving Mongaguá. Several smaller UFOs were heading towards the "flying square" on random trajectories.
Around 6 PM on June 21, the same Mr. Walter Cunha mentioned earlier, saw an UFO identical to his previous sightings heading towards Praia Grande. Mrs. Branca Ramos, from Praia Grande, informed the Guarujá UFO Group (GUG) that she saw the object around 6:30 PM. A radio journalist from Rádio Guarujá also observed strange lights on the same date.
On the date of the worldwide celebration of the 50th anniversary of the beginning of the Modern Era of Flying Saucers, June 24, around 5:40 PM, Mr. Mauro Alex Penteado and many others observed an UFO that descended vertically towards the Serra, in Caraguatatuba. The object, also seen through binoculars, seemed to change size and had red, green, and white lights.
The last week of the month was dotted with reports from residents of the Lowlands. Anyone aware of other observations in the region is kindly asked to communicate with GUG - Post Office Box 039 - Guarujá - SP - 11401-970 - Brazil.

(Combat UAVs)
The United Kingdom's Defence Research and Assessment Agency (DERA) is
developing its applied study of UAVs (unmanned aerial vehicles) and expanding its work
to consider potential applications across a broad spectrum of needs for the Royal Air Force (RAF).
"Our interest (in UAVs) is significant and growing", said Michael Steeden,
head of the Air Systems sector at DERA's Defence Research Agency. Steeden
says its largest client, the Ministry of Defence (MoD), is increasingly focused on
--- seg-1f43cb53d5e77243253c42b0 [needs-review] ---
in search of future offensive air systems (FOAS). The RAF already requested last year a $58 million feasibility study on FOAS and non-traditional solutions, such as ground-controlled UAVs, with great optimism.

The United Kingdom has not been prominent in the development of UAVs. The few that exist, like the Phoenix (GEC-Marconi), are adapted for short-range performance (territorial espionage, communication systems, etc.) required by the Army or the Navy.

The DERA has an idea (illustration above) of what a low-cost, long-range, high-altitude UAV could be, with solar-powered or soil-emitted radiation-driven propellers. Apparently, new "aberrations" will soon be in the air.

(A new small planei)
The renowned scientific journal Nature
(06.6.97) reported that a microplanet was
discovered in our Solar System.
Named 1996TL66, the small planet, with a
diameter of about 450 km (an area equivalent
to the states of Bahia and Ceará combined),
is almost ten times more distant from the Sun
than Pluto. It is the brightest object found
beyond Neptune since 1978, when Charon,
Pluto's moon, was cataloged.
Jane Luu, an astronomer at the Harvard-
Smithsonian Center for Astrophysics in
Cambridge, Massachusetts, USA, and one of
the discoverers, is fascinated, as she never
imagined such a find could happen.

Hiller Flying Platform, assembled from

Department in 1953.

instinctive control (cinesthetic) when

the pilot's weight shift.

Arthur Robertson (who is cited in

built the platform.

the pilot,) and less than two meters in

without torque problems. The maximum
altitude reached by Hiller was 5m. Tests
with very UFO-suggestive results were
filmed.

the Army Air Mobility Division, where
tests continued. Currently, the platform
is at the Paul E. Garber Preservation
and Restoration Center, Maryland, USA.

(A newform of air turbulence)
In December 1992, an airplane DC-8 had one of its engines torn off while flying over the Rocky Mountains in the U.S. at an altitude of 9,900 meters. Specialized investigators concluded that the damage was caused by a violent gust of wind, relatively predictable in mountainous regions.
Calculations made by Terry Clark of the National Center for Atmospheric Research in Boulder, Colorado, revealed that the damage was caused by a new type of turbulence previously entirely unknown. It is a region with winds of highly variable speeds, spread out like a huge elongated pancake 30km long and 4km wide.

Mysterious lights over Phoenix

Something spectacular appears to have taken place in the skies of Phoenix, Arizona, USA, starting at 8:00 PM on March 13, 1997. Thousands of people witnessed lights arranged in a "V" formation slowly moving over the city. Films were made and appear to reveal that the lights were, in fact, a lighting system on the body of a gigantic and silent flying object. Peter Davenport, director of the National UFO Reporting Center in Seattle, believes the UFO could be over 3000 meters in length (!!). The immense object was featured on the front pages of news outlets like USA Today.

Other people saw isolated lights or groups of 2, 3, or more luminosities. The Luke Air Force Base was flooded with phone calls from Phoenix, Glendale, Scottsdale, and other cities requesting explanations for the phenomenon. Senator John McCain sent a letter to Major Lansford Trapp of the USAF, requesting an official investigation.

In the 50th anniversary of the Roswell Incident (July 1947), the Phoenix lights forced the American media to constantly focus on ufology. Months after the event, the topic remains prominent in both print and electronic news.

It is said that new sightings have been reported in the area.

In a request dated June 11, lawyer and ufologist Peter Gersten sought official clarification directly from the Federal Aviation Administration (FAA) under the Freedom of Information Act (FOIA). William C. Withycombe, the FAA's regional administrator, stated he found no documentation related to the case.

Researcher Richard Hoagland, known for his studies of controversial "ruins" on the Moon and Mars, believes Phoenix is located at mathematically significant geographic coordinates conducive to such a phenomenon (!).

Many speculate about the nature of the lights. Holographic projection is even mentioned. But let's wait for the official position of the USAF.

--- seg-46573148b6e1a866bc4378c8 [needs-review] ---
According to the Bolton Evening News (November 29, 1996), in February 1996, Brian White took a photograph of a strange object about 8 km northwest of Bolton, Lancashire, England. He said he only saw the aeroform upon receiving the developed photos. The record was sent to Steve Balon of the Direct Investigation Group on Aerial Phenomena (DIGAP). DIGAP claims the image shows a HALO experimental aircraft - High Altitude, Low Observable (High Altitude, Low Observable) - being secretly tested at the U.S. Air Force Base in Wharton, Lancashire.

White said he heard no sound when taking the photo, and thus would have difficulty believing it to be a triangular plane.

British Aerospace denies conducting any secret experiments, and the Ministry of Defense has even denied the existence of the HALO project. This statement is quite ridiculous, as the HALO was mentioned in the highly respected Jane's Military Aircraft.

The Ministry believes the object may be "some kind of advertising airship."

Mark Ian Birdsall, director of Quest Publications International Ltd., which publishes the excellent UFO Magazine, suggested the object could be a swan or goose, as the photo was taken near a nature reserve, but UFOlogists and bird watchers do not seem inclined to accept this hypothesis.

The truth is that military informants confirm that secret aircraft prototypes are indeed being tested. The question remains as to whether what was photographed could be one of them.

Brazilian X-Files
The Brazilian Air Force keeps documents with records of
' several cases of unidentified flying objects
--- seg-ddc99d1fe1bd15b3a1f9f87b [machine-unreviewed] ---

--- seg-e147d5668c88249a54efbd85 [needs-review] ---
There was a day when Brazil almost declared war on UFOs. It was 1986, and the country was experiencing the euphoria of the Cruzado Plan. Shortly before 8 in the evening on May 19, however, a commotion unrelated to economic matters shook the office of the then Minister of Aeronautics, Brigadier Otávio Moreira Lima. Aboard a commercial aircraft preparing to land in São José dos Campos, about 100 kilometers from São Paulo, Embraer's then president, Colonel Ozires Silva, had raised the alarm. Through the aircraft's window he had seen three moving lights on the horizon — red, green and white. "Ozires found it very strange. They certainly were not stars, nor aircraft, much less an optical illusion," says Moreira Lima, now 66, in his office at the Aeronautics Historical-Cultural Institute, which he heads in downtown Rio de Janeiro. Ozires instructed the pilot to report the incident immediately to São Paulo air traffic control at Congonhas Airport. As soon as radar confirmed the presence of at least 20 UFOs, the telephone rang on the Aeronautics Minister's desk.
"When in doubt, I activated Air Defense Command. After all, national security was at stake," recalls Moreira Lima. Two supersonic Mirage fighters took off from Anápolis Air Base (GO). Almost a thousand kilometers away, at Santa Cruz Air Base (RJ), two other aircraft, F-5s, took off. The mission's objective: pursue the UFOs. "Even if they were foreign aircraft flying over our territory without authorization, we would have had to respond promptly." But the UFOs accelerated toward the Atlantic Ocean and left our fighters behind. They disappeared without a trace.
The declaration of

The 1986 war against probable ETs is part of a secret Air Force file on UFOs, whose existence is confirmed by Brigadier Moreira Lima himself. Until the 1970s, signals appearing on radars without logical explanation were classified as "electronic anomalies," recalls former Minister Socrates Monteiro. However, in 1976, the Air Force High Command began storing reports in a secret file. Officials admit there may be more than one hundred cases. The file contains only reports from military personnel. Former civil pilot Roberto Mantovani remembers an incident in 1973 during a flight from Belém, Pará to Cayenne, French Guiana, on board a Caravelle of the now-defunct Cruzeiro do Sul airline.

--- seg-65bcf32278788bfbc6637725 [machine-unreviewed] ---
Do: Encarrado do Posto Um
Ao: Sr. Diretor-Executivo do C P B V
Assunto: Ocorrência - Comunicação.
--- seg-1c9138e4328617ec73fce610 [needs-review] ---
Around 21:30hs on Thursday, 10/07/97, the military personnel on duty at this Post — 3S Rodrigues (BINFA 6), CB Edmo (1º GCC), S2 Ramos (BINFA 6) and S2 Guerra (BINFA 6) — observed a silent object with rotating red and blue light at a distance of approximately 800m. When CB Edmo pointed his flashlight toward it, the object descended; at treetop height only a swaying red light remained, until it disappeared among the trees.
It appeared a second time at 11:40hs that same night, at a distance of approximately 500m, very bright in front of the Post, crossing BR-163 and then beside this Post and BR-163 in the direction from Post I to the Test Range. Several radio calls were made to inform GEICAMP of the incident, but contact was not established. At the time of the last appearance, a truck was being identified. Its 03 (three) occupants also saw the moving light and were asked to report it at the Test Range, because the radio was not working at that moment.

Subject: The incident - Communication.
--- seg-6f640cfc9e9333a14a9e5654 [needs-review] ---
On the seventeenth day of the month of July of the current year, this Official was notified that two military personnel on duty at Post P I, S2 Souto and S2 Ramos, observed again an UNIDENTIFIED FLYING OBJECT (UFO).

2. This event occurred around four o'clock and fifteen minutes on the sixteenth day of July of the current year. The UFO appeared at a distance of approximately two hundred meters, in a diagonal with the house (Post P I), rising to the west and disappearing to the northwest. Its movement formed an "L" and its characteristics were:

- Shape - Oval;
- Color - Azure red, which spun; emitting a strong, yellow light directed at the Post.
--- seg-3c1e5212808edbc8cc8b522d [machine-unreviewed] ---
3. No alteration was observed at any time in the terrain or in the vegetation around PI Post.

--- seg-1ebf063e99568b99edca8a08 [needs-review] ---
Edison Boaventura Junior [flugOcamer.com.br]

--- seg-b6b47549799d622e919c8cf0 [needs-review] ---
Dear members of CECOMSAER's Journalism Division,
Since your Division is responsible for monitoring and analyzing press reports of interest to the Ministry of Aeronautics, we request confirmation of the account broadcast on the television program Fantástico on July 20, 1997. In it, retired Aeronautics Colonel Uyrange Soares de Holanda describes the Brazilian Air Force's (FAB) serious involvement in UFO research, including projects such as Operation Prato in Belém do Pará and Maranhão.
We would like to know whether the cancellation of RMA 205-1 (Regulations for Safeguarding Classified Matters, dated 07/01/1977) will open the way to a UFO research project involving civilians and military personnel, along the lines of SIOANI (which operated from 1969 to 1972) or Uruguay's present CRIDOVNI-Comision Receptora e Investigadora de Denuncia de Objetos Voladores No identificados (created on August 7, 1979, by order nr. 1873 of the Uruguayan Air Force General Command).
We would also like clarification of a persistent question concerning the well-known May 1986 case, when Brigadier Octávio Júlio Moreira Lima, former Minister of Aeronautics, stated on television that Brazil "detected, recorded on radar and sent combat fighters after more than 2 dozen UFOs located in Rio and SP."
It is disconcerting that the Ministry of Aeronautics has addressed the MAIO/86 case in two completely different ways. Let us examine them:
On September 18, 1991, the Aeronautics Social Communication Center, through Carta nr. 042/DJR addressed to Mr. Rafael Cury of Curitiba-PR, reported that the episode had been extensively investigated and "the conclusions led us to magnetic anomalies that became 'plots'." It also said that "the entire military apparatus for defending the airspace was mobilized without any actual visual contact that would justify the presence of those 'plots'." In this instance an explanation was provided by Brig-do-Ar Fernando Mendes Nogueira, who signed the letter, a copy of which we possess.
On July 17, 1996, the same Aeronautics Social Communication Center, through Carta nr. 669/DRP addressed to engineer Claudeir Covo of São Paulo, stated that "after the event of May 19, 1986 was exhaustively examined and everyone involved was heard, it was not possible to reach a conclusion, and for this reason the promised disclosure did not occur. Since then, this Ministry has considered the matter closed." The letter is signed by Ten-Cel-Av. Jader da Silva Garcia, and we possess a copy.
What, then, is happening at the Ministry of Aeronautics that it is not speaking with one voice? Why was it said in 91 that there was no visual contact, when the pilots' televised testimonies confirm that there was? Former Petrobras president Ozires Silva himself, together with Captain Almyr aboard a Xingu aircraft, stated that he had visual contact. Why so much contradiction?

--- seg-651214bb4017380273ff7a2e [needs-review] ---
In every corner of the state, calls pour in to the known service, Disque-UFO (532-4008). Dozens of calls, particularly from Ibicaral, Coaraci, and tbicui, where drivers report being followed by supposed ships. The ufologist Emanuel Paranhos, responsible for Disque-UFO, in Boquira, in the state's center, also flagged mysterious lights following a craft at night, after the events of Friday. "A phone call came in," the UFO researcher says, "as well as reports that have reached us from the south of the state, where appearances have been constant for more than 15 days." One account states that a bus carrying students from Nazaré das Farinhas was followed by UFOs between Elislo Medrado and Santo Antônio de Jesus.

New reports have reached A TARDE's editorial office about the north of the last Friday. One of them, via the Internet, sent by engineer Nelson Conrado de A Filio, states that "the object skied the sky at 7:05 pm and was also observed by me at the port as it moved towards the north. What caused curiosity was the long tail of the object and the fact that it did not disintegrate before hitting the ground, being a rare and striking phenomenon."

--- seg-6a6708ecdc5da8c28c4f2660 [needs-review] ---
Subject: São Paulo, November 1997.
To: Comdabra>Brasília, DF
--- seg-008c59cf5780042b9cb6ced9 [needs-review] ---
Subject:       Operation Plate, UFO
                        Col. Uyrangê Hollanda
--- seg-a9ea75b823f50d298c39a3e1 [needs-review] ---
Initially, I would like to express my highest regards and consideration, taking the opportunity to commend the work done with dedication and zeal.

What prompted me to write this is the astonishment caused by the interview Colonel Uyraogê Hollanda granted to ufologists Gevaerd and Petit, which I just watched on video. Despite containing already known accounts regarding the so-called "Operation Plate" in the Amazon, these are stamped and endorsed by the mission chief, who stated it resulted in over 500 photographs and 4 films, 3 of which purchased by then-Captain Hollanda himself.

Adding to the impressive accounts are pages and pages of mission reports, complete with maps, sketches, and testimonies of all sorts of occurrences involving unidentified flying objects (UFOs) in the lower Brazilian airspace, which brought great fear to that population due to the consequences of such phenomena.

I recall the incident with the Tucano plane from the "Fumaça Squadron" in Santos, where amateur yet real films show an unknown aerial form at the time of the accident. For me, this raises a question for which I do not see a plausible answer, or by denying the facts, aren't we in a way fueling unnecessary speculations?

Furthermore, knowing that the UFO subject arouses scorn in the population, with those who believe in them being labeled as lunatics, why hide or rather, refuse to disclose documents, at least to those who seek them, whether they are ufologists or not, since no one else will believe, and the opinion will be that it's just the ramblings of a lunatic and will end there. Never will the FAB. the COMD AB RA, or the Ministry of Aeronautics, suffer any damage to their images, as for the majority of the Brazilian population, UFOs are a farcical matter.

Under no circumstances do I intend to disrespect opinions, but access to the photographs and films mentioned by Colonel Hollanda would bring great relief to those interested in the subject.

When I heard Colonel Hollanda describing a UFO sighted near Belém, I visualized films made around the world, such as in Mexico-Miami, Germany, showing the same UFO model, where cameramen did not know each other and never believed in UFOs. Indeed, in March 1996, in Duque de Caxias, Rio de Janeiro, a film showing this model was taken by a cameraman filming a lunar eclipse.

Therefore, the description of the UFO sighted by Colonel Hollanda is completely consistent with these others captured at different times, in different cities, in different countries.

===== PAGE 93 [page-8caaffa50e6e083ab74ebbf0] =====
--- seg-74e0189f95f9f2ba43015783 [needs-review] ---
Thus, not wanting to further take up the time of those kindly reading this, I request
permission to access the material collected in the aforementioned "Operation Plate" (i.e.,
photos and films) and any other material that may be made available.
I can assure you that my interest is not based on fanaticism, occultism, or religion, but on
the possibility of the existence of intelligent life outside of Earth, since intelligent life
does not yet exist on Earth in the intrinsic meaning of the word. I reaffirm that the majority
of the population has such basic and primary needs that this subject would go completely
unnoticed, dispelling the mistaken idea of panic and any damage to social fabric. This
documentation will only circulate among a restricted group of dedicated individuals.
Evidence of this is that some photos have been circulating for years (taken during the
aforementioned Operation Plate), and everything remains very calm, with no credit given to
them or to any other type of evidence related to the UFO subject. For the majority, it's all
just fraud, superstition, or a montage.
In conclusion, I would like to reiterate the promises made at the beginning of this letter
and thank in advance for any positive response that I eagerly await.
A hug,
Dr. Paulo Cesar Duarte Capella.
Av. Guilherme Dumont Villares, l 136. Bloco 02. Apto 34.
Morumbi. São Paulo/SP. IS105640-9O0. »(011) 844 4147.

Brazilian and foreign ufologists, from 19 nations, from all continents, gathered at the First World UFO Forum, from December 7th to 14th, 1997, at the World Parliament of the Ecumenical Fraternity, ParlaMundi of the LBV, in Brasília, Brazil, present the following facts to the Brazilian Minister of Aeronautics:

1.
--- seg-5db6503092b04ab5c649e146 [needs-review] ---
It is common knowledge that the UFO Phenomenon, represented by constant visits of space vehicles to our Planet Earth, is genuine and thus has been independently confirmed by civilian ufologists and military authorities worldwide over the past 50 years.
--- seg-2ed31f3133abe1698a1039c3 [machine-unreviewed] ---
2.
--- seg-25e9d0da2c8c00c2643a1351 [machine-unreviewed] ---
What such phenomenon has already had its origin fully identified as
being extraterrestrial and that the vehicles that visit us so insistently
-
- come from civilizations technologically more advanced than ours, but
that coexist with us in the Universe.
--- seg-05709744b5d38d6c339fcf62 [machine-unreviewed] ---
%
--- seg-b45079fcf52b965c3d6eb138 [machine-unreviewed] ---
3. - These civilizations are in a continuous process of approaching Earth and our planetary civilization. Similarly, in their maneuvers, these civilizations, in the vast majority of cases, do not demonstrate hostility towards us.
4.
--- seg-6c17109dc7aab58d2897d21e [machine-unreviewed] ---
That visits from such extraterrestrial civilizations to Earth have increased, gradually, in recent years, as confirmed by national and international statistics, both in quantity and in depth and intensity.
--- seg-511ec7d93c6930145b0fd1b4 [machine-unreviewed] ---
5.
--- seg-8b74a0d0c23dacaaceeae649 [machine-unreviewed] ---
